Conclusion: FLUX3D通过解决表示瓶颈和跨模态对齐瓶颈，有效地提升了基于稀疏体素的图像转3DGS生成质量，证明了所提方法在可扩展高质量3D内容生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse voxel representation has emerged as a scalable foundation for image-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation, yet current methods struggle to preserve high-frequency visual details of input images due to two structural bottlenecks. First, they adopt discriminative 2D features optimized for semantic abstraction to construct sparse voxel latents, which suppress reconstructive cues and induce a representation bottleneck. Second, in the generation stage, standard diffusion transformers lack effective mechanisms to align dense 2D image tokens with sparse 3D voxel latents, resulting in a cross-modal correspondence bottleneck. To address these issues, we propose FLUX3D, a scalable image-to-3DGS framework that boosts both representation learning and cross-modal alignment during generation. We first revisit 2D feature selection for sparse-voxel-based 3D representation learning, propose Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) and couple it with a decoder-only architecture to improve 3DGS reconstruction fidelity. We also design a sparse-structure-aware diffusion framework, which integrates the Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to achieve geometry-agnostic 2D-3D alignment. Extensive benchmark experiments demonstrate that FLUX3D yields substantial improvements in appearance fidelity and significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in generating high-quality 3DGS assets.

</details>


### [179] [DDStereo: Efficient Dual Decoder Transformers for Stereo 3D Road Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24805)
*Shiyi Mu,Zichong Gu,Zhiqi Ai,Yilin Gao,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDStereo，一种双解码器立体Transformer，首次实现立体3D检测的实时性能与开放集泛化能力，在精度和速度上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立体3D检测方法精度虽高于单目方法，但推理速度慢，无法实时应用；同时开放集检测在立体3D领域尚属空白。

Method: 设计双轻量化解码器架构，分别处理开放集前景2D检测和3D属性回归，通过共享目标级查询实现统一对齐；设计紧凑的视差特征提取器和精简解码器结构提升推理效率。

Result: 在公开立体3D基准上，闭集和开放集协议下均达到SOTA精度；推理速度超越现有立体3D检测器，首次实现与单目方法相当的实时性能。

Conclusion: DDStereo填补了立体3D开放集检测的空白，证明了立体3D检测在实时性和开放集泛化两方面可以兼得。

Abstract: Stereo-based 3D object detection still faces two critical safety challenges: real-time performance and open-set generalization. Existing stereo 3D methods typically achieve twice the accuracy of monocular methods but suffer from significantly lower inference speeds, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Meanwhile, recent advances in open-world detection have introduced open-set and open-vocabulary algorithms in monocular 2D and 3D settings, yet stereo-based open-set detection remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose DDStereo, a novel Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer for real-time open-set 3D object detection. DDStereo features two lightweight decoder branches: one for open-set foreground 2D detection and the other for 3D attribute regression. These decoders share object-level queries to achieve unified target-level alignment. To enhance inference efficiency, we designed a compact disparity feature extractor and a streamlined decoder architecture. Experiments on public stereo 3D benchmarks demonstrate that DDStereo achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under both closed-set and open-set protocols. Notably, our method surpasses existing stereo 3D detectors in inference speed and, for the first time, achieves real-time performance comparable to monocular approaches.

</details>


### [180] [High-Fidelity Synthetic Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Generation Using Diffusion Probabilistic Models for Data-Limited Semiconductor Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24817)
*Johannes Boehm,Bappaditya Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的框架，在极少量样本（仅15张）条件下合成透射电子显微镜（TEM）图像，解决了半导体节点中数据稀缺问题，并支持下游机器学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进半导体节点对TEM需求激增，但破坏性制样、成像缓慢和高成本导致可用于机器学习的多样化数据集严重不足，亟需高质量合成数据生成方法。

Method: 采用DDPM框架，通过渐进式分块训练策略（从低分辨率块到全图）、定制化TrivialAugment适配、跨工艺域迁移、分类器引导和RePaint式填充，实现仅用15个样本从头训练；并利用DDPM编码器特征图分割获得区域掩码。

Result: 合成图像MS-SSIM > 0.98，专家评估与结构相似性结果一致，有效支持缺陷检测、分割和计量等下游ML任务。

Conclusion: 该DDPM框架能在极端数据稀缺条件下生成统计和物理真实的TEM图像，满足FAB计量要求，为半导体领域的机器学习提供了可行的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Advanced semiconductor nodes drastically increased demand for Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), yet destructive sample preparation, slow imaging and high costs severely limit the availability of diverse datasets needed for downstream machine learning (ML). Synthetic data generation is becoming essential, but current generative models often miss TEM-specific noise, structural detail, and stochastic variability crucial for evaluation. We present a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) framework for synthetic TEM image generation under extreme data scarcity. A progressive patch-based training strategy scales from low-resolution patches to full images, enabling from-scratch training with only 15 samples. We integrate a custom TrivialAugment adaptation, cross-process domain transfer, classifier guidance, and RePaint-style inpainting, culminating in full-image generation that preserves global structural and spatial relationships in compliance with FAB metrology requirements. Beyond synthesis, we repurpose DDPM feature representations for segmentation, partitioning encoder feature maps to obtain coherent region masks. Our synthetic images achieve up to MS-SSIM > 0.98 and qualitative expert assessment consistent with structural similarity results, facilitating downstream ML training for defect detection, segmentation, and metrology while preserving statistical and physical realism.

</details>


### [181] [GeoT2V-Bench: Benchmarking 3D Consistency in Text-to-Video Models via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24829)
*Chenrui Fan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了GeoT2V-Bench基准，用于评估相机提示的文生视频(T2V)模型能否生成支持刚性3D重建的视频，通过重建轮廓分析发现不同指标常存在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相机提示T2V模型生成的视频在视觉上可能看似合理，但无法保证帧间能够提供一致的多视角证据来支撑静态3D场景重建，因此需要一种诊断基准来系统评估其3D重建能力。

Method: 构建GeoT2V-Bench流水线：使用VGGT风格几何估计预测每帧相机内参和位姿，拟合DeformableGS，通过时间中值聚合得到静态MedianGS代理，沿估计相机路径渲染该代理，输出连续重建轮廓（包括图像运动、轨迹行为、静态渲染误差、流一致性和灵活vs静态拟合差距）。

Result: 在3,840次重建（12个开源模型配置×80个静态场景提示×4个种子）上，发现可见运动、静态渲染误差、流一致性和灵活vs静态行为经常不一致，说明不同指标捕捉了互补的失败模式。

Conclusion: GeoT2V-Bench揭示了T2V视频作为全局静态场景采集时出现的互补性失败模式，为评估生成视频的3D一致性提供了多维度的诊断工具。

Abstract: Camera-prompted text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to synthesize virtual camera captures, such as orbiting objects or moving through static scenes. For these outputs, visual plausibility is insufficient: the generated frames should also provide coherent multi-view evidence for a single static 3D scene. We introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether camera-prompted T2V clips can support explicit rigid 3D reconstruction. Our pipeline estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses with VGGT-style geometry estimation, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy by temporal-median aggregation, and renders this proxy along the estimated camera path. Instead of producing a pass/fail label or a single scalar score, GeoT2V-Bench reports a continuous reconstruction profile covering apparent image motion, estimated trajectory behavior, MedianGS static rendering error, static-render flow agreement, and the gap between flexible and static fits. On a fair-format four-seed evaluation with 3,840 completed reconstructions from 12 open-weight model configurations and 80 GeCo-Eval static-scene prompts, we find that visible motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-vs-static behavior often disagree. GeoT2V-Bench therefore captures complementary failure modes that emerge when generated videos are tested as global static-scene acquisitions.

</details>


### [182] [Bridging the Manifold Gap: Riemannian Residual Line Search for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24844)
*Hongzhu Yi,Zhongtian Luo,Tong Li,Yiyan Fan,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼残差线搜索方法，将一步扩散编辑中"强编辑"与"保源图"的矛盾转化为后验候选选择问题，通过估计提示差场的局部时间曲率构建更强编辑，再从源图到该编辑形成残差路径，最终通过CLIP对齐选择最佳候选，在PIE-Bench++上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步扩散编辑器速度快但面临根本矛盾：单次传输更新必须足够激进以实现目标提示，又必须足够保守以保留源图像，而固定更新强度无法同时满足所有编辑类型的要求。

Method: 提出黎曼残差线搜索方法：1) 估计提示差场的局部时间曲率，投影回原始一阶能量场传输估计的更新范数以构建更强编辑；2) 从源图到该强编辑形成小残差路径；3) 保留原始一阶输出作为候选之一，通过最大化目标提示的CLIP对齐度选择最终图像。

Result: 在包含10种编辑类型的700样本PIE-Bench++评估中，该方法在所有当前一步更新算法中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 将编辑强度与保真度的权衡重新定义为后验候选选择问题而非设计新的编辑模型，是一种有效且通用的改进策略。

Abstract: One-step diffusion editors are fast because they avoid inversion and iterative optimization, but a single transport update must be aggressive enough to realize the target prompt and conservative enough to preserve the source image--and no fixed update strength satisfies both demands across edit types. We treat this tension as a post-hoc candidate-selection problem on top of energy-field transport rather than as a new editing model. Our proposed method, Riemannian Residual Line Search, first builds a stronger edit by estimating the local time curvature of the prompt-delta field and projecting the corrected direction back onto the update norm of the original first-order energy-field transport estimation. It then forms a small residual path from the source image to this strong edit, retains the original first-order output as one candidate, and picks the final image by maximizing target-prompt CLIP alignment. On a 700-sample PIE-Bench++ evaluation across 10 edit type IDs, our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among current one-step update algorithms.

</details>


### [183] [Spherical-to-ERP Epipolar Rectification for Single-Axis Disparity in 360 Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24847)
*Sahereh Obeidavi,Dieter Landes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明，通过球面到等距柱状投影（ERP）预处理，将球面立体图像的二维极线位移恢复为一维视差结构，结合RAFT光流与极线对齐通道选择（EACS）框架，可在合成鱼眼立体数据集上实现实时、准确且结构一致的视差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面立体图像的全向感知特性违背了经典视差估计的几何假设——极线对应沿球面大圆路径产生二维位移，无法在几何校正前被当作单轴视差处理，因此需要探索如何将现有立体管道扩展到360°成像场景。

Method: 采用球面到等距柱状投影（ERP）作为预处理步骤，将弯曲的极线拉直并恢复一维视差结构（水平基线为水平视差，垂直基线为垂直视差）；随后使用RAFT密集光流，仅保留与基线对齐的光流分量以缩减为视差；并结合先前提出的极线对齐通道选择（EACS）框架。

Result: 在合成鱼眼立体数据集上，该球形→ERP→RAFT+EACS管道能够生成准确、平滑且结构一致的视差图，且达到实时处理速度。

Conclusion: 已建立的ERP预处理可与RAFT+EACS方法有效结合，为从球面立体图像进行实用、可解释且高效的视差估计提供了直接途径，从而将传统立体管道扩展到360°成像领域。

Abstract: Omnidirectional stereo images provide full-surround perception but violate the geometric assumptions of classical disparity estimation: in spherical or fisheye views, epipolar correspondences follow curved great-circle paths, producing two-dimensional displacements that cannot be treated as single-axis disparity before geometric rectification. In this work, we adopt a standard spherical-to-equirectangular (ERP) projection as a preprocessing step, which straightens epipolar curves and restores a one-dimensional disparity structure - horizontal for left-right rigs and vertical for top-bottom rigs. Building on our previously introduced RAFT + Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection (EACS) framework, originally developed for rectilinear and ERP stereo, we examine whether the same modular pipeline remains accurate when the input originates from spherical stereo imagery. After ERP projection, dense optical flow from RAFT is reduced to disparity by retaining only the baseline-aligned flow component. Experiments on synthetic fisheye stereo datasets show that this spherical-to-ERP-to-RAFT+EACS pipeline produces accurate, smooth, and structurally consistent disparity maps at real-time speed. These findings confirm that established ERP preprocessing can be effectively combined with our earlier RAFT+EACS method to enable practical, interpretable, and efficient disparity estimation from spherical stereo, providing a straightforward pathway for extending conventional stereo pipelines to 360 imaging.

</details>


### [184] [FLAT: Feedforward Latent Triangle Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24876)
*Orest Kupyn,Goutam Bhat,Philipp Henzler,Fabian Manhardt,Christian Rupprecht,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLAT首次从视频扩散潜变量中直接解码出三角形splat（三角面片）作为显式表面基元，相比3D高斯在几何精度上显著提升，并支持实时渲染和游戏引擎集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈场景解码器通常输出体素化3D高斯，缺乏明确定义的表面，无法直接用于仿真或标准图形管线。本文希望将压缩视频扩散潜变量直接映射为显式表面基元。

Method: 提出FLAT框架，包含两个关键设计：1）基于射线中心的旋转参数化（ray-centered rotation parameterization）用于三角形回归；2）新颖的乘积窗函数（product window function）改善可微三角形渲染中的梯度流动。

Result: 在标准基准上，FLAT在几何精度上显著优于现有前馈基线，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量；轻量级测试时间精炼可将三角形soup转换为全不透明、支持实时渲染的游戏引擎可用的表示。

Conclusion: 首次证明可以从视频扩散潜变量中直接解码三角形splat，且在相同训练设置下对3DGS、2DGS和三角形splatting变体进行了系统性的表示权衡分析。

Abstract: Generating explorable 3D scenes from a single image requires strong generative priors and accurate geometric representations suitable for downstream use. Current video diffusion models offer high-quality generation and implicitly encode multi-view geometric structure in latent space. However, existing feedforward latent scene decoders typically output volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack a well-defined surface, limiting their use in simulation or standard graphics pipelines. This motivates decoding surface-aligned primitives that are not only renderable but also closer to explicit geometric assets. We ask whether compressed video diffusion latents can be mapped directly to explicit surface primitives in a single pass. To this end, we introduce FLAT and, for the first time, show that triangle splats can be decoded directly from video diffusion latents. Compared with decoding 3D Gaussians, predicting flat primitives is notoriously more challenging due to high sensitivity to primitive orientations, oftentimes leading to poor gradient flow. FLAT solves with two key ingredients: a ray-centered rotation parameterization for triangle regression and a novel product window function that improves gradient flow during differentiable triangle rendering. On standard benchmarks, FLAT achieves significantly better geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality compared to state-of-the-art feedforward baselines. We further show that a lightweight test-time refinement step converts the predicted triangle soup into a fully opaque, game-engine-ready representation that supports real-time rendering. By evaluating 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting variants under an identical training setup, we provide the first systematic analysis of representation tradeoffs in feedforward scene generation. The project page is available at https://flat-splat.github.io

</details>


### [185] [BenchX: Benchmarking AI Models for Cancer Detection and Localization with Demographic and Protocol Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24883)
*Qi Chen,Wenxuan Li,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Xinze Zhou,Jakob Wasserthal,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci,Sezgin Er,Ashwin Kumar,Yiwen Ye,Yuhan Wang,Yuyin Zhou,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Alan L. Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模开放基准测试，包含85,355次CT扫描，系统评估了12个肿瘤检测AI模型在不同肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚组和成像协议下的表现。利用大语言模型自动提取亚组信息，发现当前最先进的AI模型在罕见或代表性不足的亚组（如年轻、非裔美国女性）中表现较差，强调了在医学影像中进行亚组级评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医学影像中跨临床环境表现不一致，尤其在患者人口统计学和成像协议变化时（如检测小肿瘤、不同对比阶段扫描、不同年龄或性别患者），现有模型缺乏系统性、大规模评估其亚组性能差异。

Method: 构建包含85,355次CT扫描的大规模开放基准，系统评估12个肿瘤检测AI模型；利用大语言模型从临床数据中提取和组织亚组信息，实现可扩展且可复现的分析。

Result: 当前最先进的AI模型在优化平均精度的同时，在罕见或代表性不足的亚组（如年轻、非裔美国女性）中表现较差，且收集这些罕见病例的充分标注数据往往不切实际。

Conclusion: 该基准为构建更可靠、更稳健的肿瘤检测AI模型提供了基础，并强调了在医学影像和计算机视觉中进行严格的亚组级评估的必要性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved remarkable success in medical imaging, but it is widely recognized that these models often perform inconsistently across real-world clinical settings. Such inconsistencies occur when patient demographics and imaging protocols vary, for example, in detecting small tumors, analyzing scans from different contrast phases, or evaluating patients of different ages or sexes. To quantify these inconsistencies, we develop a large-scale, open benchmark of 85,355 CT scans that systematically evaluates 12 tumor-detection AI models across tumor size, location, patient subgroup, and imaging protocol. We leverage large language models (LLMs) to extract and organize subgroup information from clinical data, which makes the analysis both scalable and reproducible. Our benchmark reveals that current state-of-the-art AI models, optimized for average accuracy, perform poorly in rare or underrepresented subgroups, such as young, female African Americans. However, collecting sufficient annotated data for these rare cases is often impractical. The benchmark provides a foundation for building more reliable and robust AI models for tumor detection and highlighting the need for rigorous, subgroup-level evaluation in medical imaging and computer vision. Datasets, code

</details>


### [186] [DiffusionBench: On Holistic Evaluation of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24888)
*Xingjian Leng,Jaskirat Singh,Zhanhao Liang,Ethan Smith,Martin Bell,Aninda Saha,Yuhui Yuan,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NanoGen统一框架，发现基于ImageNet的类条件生成评估与文生图(T2I)任务之间无显著相关性（Pearson相关系数-0.377至-0.580），因此构建了涵盖两个任务的DiffusionBench基准，建议替代仅用ImageNet评估的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DiT研究过度依赖ImageNet类条件生成这一单一评估设置，方法在FID等指标上虽有提升，但无法反映生成建模的真实进步。研究者因T2I训练和评估成本高而常跳过该任务，但这种认知已不再成立。

Method: 提出NanoGen统一框架，支持RAE、VAE、pixel-space和MeanFlow四种扩散方法，仅需12行配置变更即可在ImageNet和T2I间切换。在该框架下训练了21个潜在扩散模型，计算Pearson相关系数分析两类任务间方法排名的相关性，并构建DiffusionBench综合基准。

Result: 发现ImageNet与T2I生成任务间方法排名无强相关性，三个指标上的Pearson相关系数在-0.377到-0.580之间，表明在ImageNet上改进FID的方法在T2I上可能无对应提升。

Conclusion: DiT研究需同时在ImageNet和T2I两项任务上评估，建议采用DiffusionBench替代仅依靠ImageNet的评估方式，在该基准上取得改进的方法更可能反映生成建模的广泛进步。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Cryptographic certificates of validity for trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23768)
*Murdoch J. Gabbay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种为AI智能体系统颁发密码学有效性证书的方法：将策略条件形式化为逻辑谓词，编译为多项式约束的见证检查问题，并用简洁的密码学证明系统（可选零知识）来证明条件成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI智能体的形式化代码验证与密码学认证之间寻求一个中间方案，使得智能体的行为可被独立验证，而无需信任智能体本身或重新执行其计算。

Method: 将正确性或策略条件形式化为逻辑谓词，编译为多项式约束上的见证检查问题，然后使用简洁的密码学证明系统（如SNARKs）生成证书，可选地支持零知识证明。

Result: 给出了该方法的高层概述、核心数学翻译，并将其与证明携带代码、zkVM、形式化方法和智能体治理等现有工作相关联。

Conclusion: 该方法为智能体行为认证提供了可行路径，但完整的实现仍需解决规范制定、审计和部署等关键问题。

Abstract: We propose cryptographic certificates of validity for agentic AI systems. The core idea is to formally specify a correctness or policy condition as a logical predicate, compile this predicate to a witness-checking problem over polynomial constraints, and use a succinct cryptographic proof system (and optionally zero-knowledge) to certify that the condition holds.
  This offers a middle ground between formal verification of source code, and cryptographic authentication. An agent's action can be accompanied by an independently checkable proof that it satisfies an agreed formal policy, without requiring the verifier to trust the agent or to re-execute computation. We outline the approach at a high level, give the core mathematical translation, relate the proposal to proof-carrying code, zkVMs, formal methods, and agent governance, and note the specification, auditing, and deployment questions that a full implementation must answer.

</details>


### [188] [AutoPRAC: Automating Attack Discovery for PRAC-Based Rowhammer Defenses using Model Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23905)
*Joyce Qu,Gururaj Saileshwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AutoPRAC提出首个基于模型检验器的自动化测试框架，用于验证DDR5 PRAC规范的Rowhammer防御安全性，并在MOAT防御中发现了一个允许34次激活超过阈值的未报告漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PRAC设计的安全性评估依赖人工构造的攻击模式，缺乏形式化验证手段来保证安全属性，也缺少自动化检测实现缺陷的技术。

Method: 将PRAC实现建模为有界状态机，针对最坏情况下的预言机攻击者检验安全关键属性，若违反则生成具体的反例攻击轨迹。

Result: 在MOAT防御中发现了其计数器重置策略的缺陷，可导致多达34次激活超出Rowhammer阈值未被检测到。

Conclusion: AutoPRAC能够自动发现Rowhammer缓解措施中的细微安全缺陷，可作为PRAC设计的早期辅助工具用于攻击发现。

Abstract: Per-Row Activation Counting (PRAC) in DDR5 is a specification to mitigate Rowhammer attacks by tracking activations per row and triggering mitigative refreshes when needed. However, the security of PRAC designs is currently evaluated using human-crafted attack patterns and we lack formal verification of their security properties, or automated techniques to detect implementation flaws. In this work, we present AutoPRAC, the first automated technique to test the security of PRAC-based defenses using model checkers. AutoPRAC models PRAC implementations as bounded state machines and checks security-critical safety properties against a worst-case oracle attacker. If a property is violated, the framework produces a concrete counterexample trace corresponding to a successful attack. Using AutoPRAC, we uncover a previously unreported flaw in MOAT, a state-of-the-art PRAC defense, in its counter-reset policy that allows up to 34 activations to go undetected above the Rowhammer threshold. Our results demonstrate that AutoPRAC can automatically discover subtle security flaws in Rowhammer mitigations and serves as an early-stage design aid for attack discovery on PRAC designs.

</details>


### [189] [BipBipCache: Pipeline-Aware Integration of Low-Latency Tweakable Encryption in an Embedded Cache Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23941)
*Corbin Hibler,Firas Hassan,Eric McKanna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BipBipCache是一种直接映射缓存控制器，集成了BipBip可调分组密码，用于实时加密缓存数据和标签，通过3周期解密器与6周期加密器的流水线协同设计，有效隐藏了加密延迟，在Xilinx Artix-7上验证了硬件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级和嵌入式处理器中片上SRAM缓存存储的敏感数据在断电或物理探测后仍然可读，现有方案难以兼顾安全性与性能开销，需要一种高效的缓存数据实时加密方案来抵御冷启动攻击、总线攻击和SRAM读出攻击。

Method: 提出BipBipCache控制器，集成BipBip TBC密码，采用C$^3$风格的24+40位分解每个64位字；从仅解密器规格重构了首个流水线硬件BipBip加密器，并与3周期解密器在缓存数据路径中协同设计；利用加密器前3个流水阶段与标签解密和命中检测重叠，将有效写入惩罚从6周期降至3周期。

Result: 在Xilinx Artix-7上占用3,356个LUT（器件16.1%，加密逻辑约占79%）；通过官方BipBip C++参考的五组向量验证加密器与解密器正确性；在硬件上确认端到端运行。

Conclusion: 6周期的加密延迟通过流水线重叠并未完全转化为6周期写入惩罚，有效写入提交仅需3周期，证明了实时缓存加密在可接受面积开销下的可行性。

Abstract: Consumer and embedded processors store sensitive data in on-chip SRAM caches that remain readable after power loss or physical probing unless ciphertext is maintained in the memory array itself. This paper presents BipBipCache, a direct-mapped cache controller that integrates the BipBip tweakable block cipher (TBC) to encrypt cache data and tags in real time using a C$^3$-style 24+40 bit decomposition of each 64-bit word. We reconstruct the first pipelined hardware BipBip encryptor from a decryptor-centric specification and coordinate it with a 3-cycle decryptor inside the cache datapath.
  Our threat model targets confidentiality of cache-resident contents against cold-boot, bus, and SRAM readout attacks. A key architectural result is that 6-cycle encryption latency does not fully translate into 6-cycle write penalty: the first three encryptor stages overlap with tag decryption and hit detection, leaving an effective 3-cycle write commitment after hit verification. We verify encryptor and decryptor correctness against the official BipBip C++ reference (five vectors each), report FPGA resource utilization on Xilinx Artix-7 (3,356 LUTs, 16.1% of device; crypto logic ~79% of LUTs), and confirm end-to-end operation on hardware.

</details>


### [190] [Maestro Order: A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23983)
*Hidayet Aksu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Maestro Order框架，通过分解、集成、验证和递归四种结构原语及预算感知控制器，将不可靠的单一模型前向传播转化为可靠的解题系统，模拟验证了验证机制几何级提升可靠性（0.55→0.98/0.999），预算控制器能以远低于投票的成本达到目标可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型单次前向传播虽然快速流畅但不可靠，会产生自信的错误（幻觉），需要一种系统化的方法将这些不可靠的求解器组合成可靠的解题系统。

Method: 提出Maestro Order编排框架：（1）四种结构原语：分解（decompose）、集成（ensemble）、验证（verify）、递归（recurse）；（2）预算感知控制器，根据边际可靠性/单位成本分配验证和投票到最有效的阶段；（3）统一接口将任意模型视为黑盒基础求解器，在线测量验证器鉴别能力。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟定量验证了：验证几何级提升可靠性（两门控0.55→0.98，四门控→0.999）；投票仅在高于随机水平时有效且受限于共享错误；预算感知控制器以远低于纯投票的成本达到目标可靠性。

Conclusion: 构建鲁棒的检查器、多样化求解器、让控制器将计算资源分配到信息量最大的地方；同时指出验证器博弈、相关错误、分解错误累积等失败模式。

Abstract: A single forward pass of a capable model is a fast, fluent, and unreliable problem-solver: it is right often enough to be useful and wrong often enough to be dangerous; in language models, such confident errors are known as hallucinations. We present Maestro Order, a model-agnostic orchestration harness that turns unreliable solvers into reliable problem-solving systems by composing them according to four structural primitives (decompose, ensemble, verify, and recurse) and a budget-aware controller that decides where to spend compute. The harness treats any model as a black-box base solver behind a uniform interface, layers a verifier ensemble whose discrimination is measured online, and allocates verification and voting to the stages with the highest marginal reliability per unit cost. We give the architecture, the message and state schema, the controller algorithm, and the engineering that makes it deterministic, observable, and fault-tolerant. We then specify an evaluation methodology (reliability at fixed cost, coverage, calibration, and ablations) and report results from a faithful Monte Carlo simulation of the harness over a parameterized solver/verifier model. The simulation reproduces the predicted laws quantitatively: verification amplifies reliability geometrically (e.g. $0.55\to0.98$ with two gates, $\to0.999$ with four), voting helps only above chance and is limited by shared errors, and a budget-aware controller reaches a target reliability at a small fraction of the cost of voting alone by selecting the cheapest mechanism for each regime. We close with failure modes (verifier gaming, correlated errors, and decomposition error compounding) and concrete guidance: build robust checkers, diversify solvers, and let the controller put compute where the information is.

</details>


### [191] [PixJail: Self-Evolving Paper-to-Pipeline Reproduction for Text-to-Image Jailbreak Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24081)
*Leyi Sheng,Han Sun,Zhen Sun,Yuntao Yue,Jinlin Wu,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PixJail，一个自演进的论文到流水线智能体框架，用于可复现的文本到图像越狱评估。该框架能够根据论文自动构建攻击模块和可运行评估流水线，在统一协议下忠实复现原始实验结果，并维护记忆库以支持未来复现工作。在11种代表性方法上实现了平均2.1%的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I越狱技术发展迅速，但基准测试和复现工作流难以跟上步伐。T2I越狱评估不是单轮提示测试，而是涉及提示变换、图像生成、安全过滤和多模态判决等多个阶段的流水线级问题，导致不同论文的结果难以可靠复现和公平比较。

Method: 提出PixJail框架，包含：(1) 论文特定攻击模块构建器，可基于论文和可选参考代码自动构建；(2) 统一协议下的可运行评估流水线；(3) 记忆库，存储论文摘要、攻击演化模式、可复用模板、失败案例和版本化工件，支持经验复用。

Result: 复现了11种代表性T2I越狱方法（包括有代码和无代码论文），在原始设置下以平均2.1%的误差（中位数0%）准确恢复先前结果。

Conclusion: PixJail可作为未来T2I越狱复现和评估的统一基础，显著减少人工工作量。

Abstract: As Text-to-Image (T2I) jailbreak techniques evolve rapidly, existing benchmarks and reproduction workflows often struggle to keep pace. More importantly, T2I jailbreak evaluation is not a single prompt-level test, but a pipeline-level problem shaped by multiple stages, including prompt transformation, image generation, safety filtering, and multimodal judging. This makes results across papers difficult to reliably reproduce and fairly compare. To bridge this gap, we propose PixJail, a self-evolving paper-to-pipeline agent framework for reproducible T2I jailbreak evaluation. Given a T2I jailbreak paper and optional reference code, PixJail rapidly constructs a paper-specific attack module and a runnable evaluation pipeline under a unified contract, while faithfully reproducing the original experimental results. PixJail further maintains a memory bank that stores paper digests, attack evolution patterns, reusable templates, failure cases, and versioned artifacts, enabling future reproduction efforts to reuse prior experience. We reproduce eleven representative T2I jailbreak methods, including both code-available and code-unavailable papers. Under their original settings, our framework accurately recovers prior results with minimal error (2.1\% average, 0\% median). We hope that PixJail can serve as a unified foundation for future T2I jailbreak reproduction and evaluation, significantly reducing manual effort.

</details>


### [192] [DoHFuse: A Dual-Branch Architecture with DMAGLSTM for Website Fingerprinting over DNS over HTTPS/3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24105)
*ZunDong Zhang,Yanan Cheng,Zhaoxin Zhang,Xueyang Huo,Changjiang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了DNS over HTTP/3（DoH/3）协议在物联网和边缘网络环境中对网站指纹攻击的脆弱性。作者收集并发布了首个真实世界的DoH/3流量基准数据集，并提出了DoHFuse双分支学习框架（结合改进的DMAG-LSTM），实验表明DoH/3流量仍易受WF攻击，表明现有填充机制不足以保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DoH/3在现实网络系统中的部署及填充策略的广泛应用，需要探究DoH/3在真实物联网和边缘网络场景中是否能有效缓解网站指纹攻击，以验证现有隐私保护机制的有效性。

Method: 收集并发布首个真实世界DoH/3流量基准数据集；提出DoHFuse双分支学习框架，通过改进的DMAG-LSTM融合到达时间间隔序列和精细化统计特征，以捕获突发对齐的时间模式。

Result: 在449类封闭世界设定下准确率达88.05%（精确率88.56，召回率87.96，F1 87.83）；在开放世界检测中AUPRC达0.975，F1达0.951（精确率0.906，召回率1.0）。

Conclusion: DoH/3流量仍易受网站指纹攻击，表明当前常用的填充机制不足以确保物联网规模加密DNS通信中的隐私保护。

Abstract: As personal data privacy becomes increasingly critical in Internet of Things (IoT) environments, secure DNS protocols such as DNS over HTTPS (DoH) and DNS over TLS (DoT) have been widely adopted to protect device communications. However, without effective obfuscation, these protocols remain vulnerable to Website Fingerprinting (WF) attacks that can reveal user activity. With the ongoing deployment of DNS over HTTP/3 (DoH/3) in modern networked systems, padding strategies have been increasingly applied in practice. It is therefore essential to investigate whether DoH/3 can effectively mitigate WF attacks in realistic IoT and edge-network scenarios. To address this, we first collect and publicly release the first real-world benchmark dataset of DoH/3 traffic, generated from domain resolution processes in practical network environments. We further propose DoHFuse, a dual-branch learning framework that integrates inter-arrival time sequences and refined statistical features through an improved DMAG-LSTM, specifically designed to capture burst-aligned temporal patterns. Experimental results show that DoHFuse achieves an accuracy of 88.05% (precision 88.56, recall 87.96, F1 87.83) in a closed-world setting of 449 classes, and an AUPRC of 0.975 with an F1 score of 0.951 (precision 0.906, recall 1.0) in open-world detection. These findings demonstrate that DoH/3 traffic remains susceptible to WF attacks, suggesting that commonly deployed padding mechanisms alone are insufficient to ensure privacy protection in IoT-scale encrypted DNS communications.

</details>


### [193] [CORE-BREW: LLR-Based Soft Decoding for Robust Multi-Bit LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24163)
*Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CORE-BREW通过恒定命中率扩展和多比特水印软判决解码，在保持严格假阳性控制的同时，提升了LLM输出水印对编辑和释义攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ECC的LLM水印依赖硬判决解码，丢弃了token级别的可靠性信息，导致在编辑和释义攻击下鲁棒性不足，且难以严格控制假阳性率。

Method: CORE-BREW提出了恒定命中率（Constant-hit-Rate）嵌入策略，通过固定命中率p-star校准水印信道，推导出闭式逐token对数似然比用于软判决解码；支持两种检测模式：Strict-Safe（保持有界距离的指定码字接受域）和FPR-Calibrated（基于似然评分和轻量级列表解码表征FPR-TPR权衡）。

Result: 在开源LLM上的实验表明，在token级编辑和释义攻击下，CORE-BREW相比以往多比特水印基线方法，在低FPR区域的辨别力和鲁棒性均有提升，同时保持了相当的语义质量。

Conclusion: CORE-BREW通过软判决解码和灵活的检测模式，有效解决了多比特水印在严格假阳性控制与鲁棒性之间的权衡问题，为LLM输出的可靠溯源提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Reliable provenance for LLM outputs requires multi-bit watermarks that remain robust under editing while maintaining strict false-positive control. Existing ECC-based LLM watermarks rely largely on hard-decision decoding, discarding token-level reliability information. We propose CORE-BREW, a Constant-hit-Rate Embedding extension of block-wise BREW for robust multi-bit watermarking. CORE-BREW calibrates the watermark channel by targeting a fixed hit rate p-star, yielding closed-form per-token log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) for principled soft-decision decoding. It supports two detection modes: Strict-Safe, which preserves the bounded-distance designated-codeword acceptance region, and FPR-Calibrated, which uses likelihood-based scoring and lightweight list decoding to characterize the FPR-TPR trade-off. Experiments on open-source LLMs under token-level edits and paraphrasing demonstrate improved low-FPR discrimination and robustness over prior multi-bit watermarking baselines while maintaining comparable semantic quality.

</details>


### [194] [Securing LLM-Agent Long-Term Memory Against Poisoning: Non-Malleable, Origin-Bound Authority with Machine-Checked Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24322)
*Yedidel Louck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM智能体长期记忆的投毒漏洞：攻击者可通过摘要重构、可信工具回显和制造佐证三条渠道"洗白"恶意记忆来源，绕过现有基于内容或谱系的防御。作者形式化证明了无防御是完备的，提出基于非可篡改信息流控制（IFC）的TMA-NM方案，在八个前沿模型上实现0%攻击成功率且不损失合法效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖持久长期记忆，但现有防御（内容检测/信任评分、谱系追踪）均可被绕过。攻击者可通过三种渠道（智能体自身摘要、可信工具回显、制造佐证）将不信任来源的记忆"洗白"为可信来源，进而操控未来会话中的支付、设置变更或数据窃取等关键行为。这一安全漏洞尚未被系统性地形式化分析和防御。

Method: 首先形式化定义了记忆写-检索-执行管道的可篡改性（malleability），并通过机器验证的TLA+模型证明了分离定理：（T1）基于内容或谱系的防御在洗白攻击下均不成立；（T2）写入时绑定来源是必要条件；（T3）非可篡改的来源绑定权限+抗Sybil的确认门控提升是充分条件。基于此构造了TMA-NM，实例化了针对LLM智能体记忆的非可篡改信息流控制（IFC），确保记忆的来源不可被篡改地绑定，且权限提升需经过抗Sybil的确认机制。

Result: 在八个前沿模型（含GPT-4、Claude等）上的跨防御、跨攻击、跨模型基准测试中：现有防御在洗白攻击下失败率高达68%，与理论预测完全吻合；TMA-NM在直接攻击和所有三种洗白渠道上均达到0%攻击成功率，且合法任务效用完全不受影响。研究团队已开源基准测试、测试框架和机器验证的TLA+模型。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的长期记忆投毒是真实且严重的安全威胁，现有内容/谱系防御本质上不可靠。非可篡改的来源绑定（即记忆写入时不可篡改地记录其来源）是对抗洗白攻击的必要且充分条件。TMA-NM通过IFC机制实现了这一设计原则，在保证安全性的同时不牺牲可用性，为构建可信的LLM智能体记忆系统提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent long-term memory, which creates a critical vulnerability that we study here: memory poisoning. An adversary can store untrusted content in one session that later steers a consequential action, such as a payment, a setting change, or data exfiltration, in a future session. Existing defenses base a memory item's authority to act on either its content (detection or trust-scoring) or its derivation history (lineage). We show that both signals are malleable. An attacker can launder an untrusted origin through three channels specific to LLM agents: the agent's own summarization, a trusted-tool echo, and manufactured corroboration. Each makes the content look benign and breaks or flips its derivation edge to ``trusted.'' We formalize malleability for the memory write-retrieve-act pipeline and prove a machine-checked separation theorem. No content- or lineage-based defense is sound under laundering (T1), write-time origin binding is necessary (T2), and non-malleable origin-bound authority with Sybil-resistant corroboration-gated elevation is sufficient (T3). Our construction, TMA-NM (Tamper-evident Memory Authority, Non-Malleable), instantiates non-malleable information-flow control (IFC) for LLM-agent memory. A cross-defense, cross-attack, and cross-model benchmark over eight frontier models shows that existing defenses fail exactly where the theory predicts (up to 68% laundering attack-success), while TMA-NM reaches 0% attack success on both direct and laundering attacks across all models and channels, at full legitimate utility. We release the benchmark, harness, and machine-checked TLA+ models to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [195] [ComputeFHE: A Privacy-Preserving General-Purpose Computation Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24379)
*Faris Serdar Tasel,Efe Ciftci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ComputeFHE是一个基于TFHE的开源C++库，提供加密整数和定点数类型及多种操作，通过优化ALU架构将引导操作减少高达3.9倍，并包含无需实际加密计算的仿真模式，用于开发隐私保护应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密(FHE)虽能直接在加密数据上执行计算，但实际应用受到高计算成本和开发复杂性的限制，亟需一个既高效又易于使用的开发框架。

Method: 基于TFHE密码系统，提供加密整数和定点数类型及算术、逻辑、比较、条件和 oblivious 数组访问操作；支持传统双输入逻辑门算术和基于FHE友好逻辑原语的优化ALU架构；包含仿真模式用于测试和复杂度分析。

Result: 优化后的ALU架构显著减少了引导操作数量，在选定操作上实现了高达3.9倍的性能提升；仿真模式能够在不进行实际加密计算的情况下提供电路复杂度和引导成本分析。

Conclusion: ComputeFHE作为一个实用且可访问的开源框架，降低了FHE应用开发门槛，为隐私保护算法和应用的开发与评估提供了有效支持。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables computations to be performed directly on encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, its practical applications remain limited by high computational costs and development complexity. This paper presents ComputeFHE, an open-source C++ library that facilitates the development of privacy-preserving applications based on the TFHE cryptosystem. The library provides encrypted integer and fixed-point data types together with arithmetic, logical, comparison, conditional, and oblivious array-access operations which allow developers to implement algorithms using a familiar imperative programming paradigm. ComputeFHE supports both conventional TFHE arithmetic based on standard two-input logic gates and an optimized Arithmetic Logic Unit (ALU) architecture utilizing FHE-friendly logic primitives. Experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in the number of required bootstrapping operations, achieving performance improvements of up to 3.9x for selected operations. In addition, the library includes a simulation mode that enables testing, debugging, and complexity analysis without performing actual cryptographic computations while providing circuit complexity and bootstrapping costs. Built on top of OpenFHE, ComputeFHE offers a practical and accessible framework for developing and evaluating privacy-preserving algorithms and applications.

</details>


### [196] [Poisoned Playbooks: Demystifying Knowledge Poisoning Effects on AI Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24402)
*Juho Park,Hyunmin Choi,Kevin Nam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RAG增强的AI安全智能体易受恶意注入的安全知识投毒攻击，系统性地改变了智能体的行为。作者在11个CTF挑战、3个前沿LLM系列、2代模型和11个真实CVE上验证了投毒的系统性采纳模式，提出了"验证边界"(VB)三级经验分类法来解释该现象，并评估了验证提示和多源检索的防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG投毒研究主要关注问答场景下的答案篡改，但对安全智能体这类需要执行具体操作（exploit行为）的代理系统受到的投毒影响知之甚少。随着AI安全智能体越来越依赖RAG获取外部安全知识进行漏洞分析和利用推理，投毒攻击风险显著增加。

Method: 作者设计了一种名为"Poisoned Playbooks"的恶意投毒手段，将构造的单一恶意文档注入公开安全知识源。在11个CTF挑战、3个前沿LLM系列（含2代模型）和11个真实CVE上验证投毒效果。提出"验证边界"(VB)三级经验分类法，用于描述智能体驳回检索到的不当信息的能力。评估了验证提示和多源检索两种防御策略。

Result: 投毒采纳表现出系统性而非随机性模式。验证提示和多源检索在存在强证据时有效，但稀疏证据和零日漏洞条件下防御能力显著减弱。

Conclusion: RAG增强的AI安全智能体面临被恶意投毒的知识源系统性操控的风险，现有防御在证据不足时难以保证安全，需要针对安全操作场景设计更鲁棒的防护机制。

Abstract: AI security agents increasingly rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to use external security knowledge for vulnerability analysis and exploit reasoning. This creates a new risk: poisoned write-ups can be operationalized into incorrect exploit behavior. Yet, prior work on RAG poisoning has mostly studied answer corruption in QA settings, much less is known about action-taking security agents. This paper aims to reveal such characteristics with crafted poisons about real-world challenges and AI agents. First, we demonstrate how a crafted single poisoned write-up injected into public-style security knowledge sources which we denote as Poisoned Playbooks, alters the behavior of RAG-based AI security agents. Across 11 CTF challenges, 3 frontier LLM families, 2 model generations, and 11 real-world CVEs, we find that poison adoption is systematic rather than random. To explain this pattern, we introduce the Verification Boundary (VB), a 3-level empirical classification based on what evidence the agent can use to refute a retrieved claim. Finally, we evaluate verification prompting and multi-source retrieval, showing that both help when stronger evidence exists, but weaken under sparse-evidence and zero-day conditions.

</details>


### [197] [A Comparison of Kubernetes Compliance Standards and Configuration Scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24438)
*Michael Krieger,Markus Gierlinger,Farooq Shaikh,Mario Kahlhofer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了8种Kubernetes加固指南，建立了79项配置建议基准，并对10种静态配置扫描工具进行了实证评估，发现各指南和工具在配置问题覆盖范围、评分和排序上存在显著差异，亟需更标准化、透明和一致的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近年来出现了多种Kubernete集群和部署的加固指南及扫描工具，但其指导建议和输出结果不一致，导致配置和优先级决策缺乏一致性，因此需要系统比较这些指南和工具，揭示其差异。

Method: 系统比较8种常用Kubernetes加固指南，结合最佳实践建立79项Kubernetes配置建议基准，并对10种流行的静态配置扫描工具及其评分输出进行结构化实证评估。

Result: 发现各加固指南和扫描工具在配置问题覆盖范围上存在显著差异，不同扫描工具对配置问题的评分和排序也存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 当前Kubernetes配置问题的风险和严重性评估需要更标准化、透明和一致的方法。

Abstract: Kubernetes has become the industry standard for orchestrating containers in microservice-based software architectures. While several hardening guidelines and scanning tools for securing Kubernetes clusters and deployments have emerged in recent years, their differing guidance and outputs often lead to inconsistent configuration and prioritization decisions. This work presents a systematic comparison of eight commonly used Kubernetes hardening guidelines. Through this comparison and the inclusion of best practices, we established a benchmark of 79 Kubernetes configuration recommendations and conducted the a structured empirical evaluation of ten popular static configuration scanning tools and their scoring outputs. Our findings reveal substantial disparities in the coverage of configuration issues across hardening guidelines and scanners, as well as inconsistencies in how configuration issues are scored and ranked by different scanners. These results highlight the need for more standardized, transparent, and consistent approaches to risk and severity assessment of Kubernetes configuration issues.

</details>


### [198] [Red-Teaming the Agentic Red-Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24496)
*Dario Pasquini,Michal Bazyli,Taras Fedynyshyn,Artem Sorokin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次深入分析了主流自主攻击安全代理系统的安全漏洞，揭示了它们在架构层面的共同设计缺陷，使得攻击者能够窃取API密钥、建立持久化后门并完全控制操作者机器（即使代理运行在沙箱容器内）。作者提出了针对此类系统的完整网络杀伤链，并给出了可缓解攻击路径的稳健架构和设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然社区致力于构建越来越强大的攻击性安全代理系统，但很少关注这些系统本身的安全性。本文旨在填补这一空白，对主流系统进行安全分析。

Method: 对最广泛使用的攻击性安全代理系统进行深入的安全分析，引入针对此类系统的完整网络杀伤链模型（涵盖LLM操控、横向移动、持久化、护栏绕过和沙箱逃逸等阶段），并基于分析结果推导出稳健的架构设计方案。

Result: 发现大多数攻击性安全代理系统存在共同的设计缺陷，使得主动攻击者能够窃取API密钥、建立持久化后门、完全控制操作者机器（即使代理运行在沙箱内）。

Conclusion: 提出了可缓解所披露攻击路径的稳健架构和可操作、广泛适用的设计原则，从架构层面提升这类系统的安全性。

Abstract: The use of agentic systems to perform offensive security operations has moved from a theoretical possibility to a commoditized capability. However, while the community has focused on creating more and more capable agents, less attention has been allocated to assessing the security of those systems.
  In this work, we present the first in-depth security analysis of the most widely used agentic systems for offensive security operations. We show that most of these tools share common design flaws that enable an active adversary to exfiltrate API keys, establish persistent footholds, and fully compromise the operator's machine, even when the agent operates inside a sandboxed container. To support our analysis, we introduce a full cyber kill chain for such agentic systems, capturing the progression from initial LLM manipulation to lateral movement, persistence, guardrail bypass, and sandbox escape.
  Building on our security analysis, we derive a robust architecture for agentic offensive-security tools and propose actionable, broadly applicable design principles that mitigate the disclosed attack paths at the architectural level.

</details>


### [199] [FirmCure:Towards Autonomous and Adaptive Rehosting of Linux-Based Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24549)
*Chuan Hong,Zheng Zhang,Lei Zhou,Laisong Li,Chenyifan Liu,Ze Huang,Xu Zhou,Peihong Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FirmCure是一个由LLM驱动的Linux固件全系统重托管框架，通过自适应感知推理、反思性合成和自主运行时干预三个模块，实现了对异构固件的自动化重托管，在网络端口开放率（100%）和服务交互性（90.5%）上显著超越现有方法，并成功复现和发现了安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固件重托管方法在处理定制化设备时，因专用架构和硬件相关配置导致初始化和运行时障碍，严重依赖专家干预，造成重托管效率低下。

Method: 提出FirmCure框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 自适应感知推理（Adaptive Perception Inference），通过静态分析提取固件结构依赖；2) 反思性合成（Reflective Synthesis），进行迭代配置优化；3) 自主运行时干预（Autonomous Runtime Intervention），通过运行时故障诊断和监控实现实时错误修复。

Result: 在来自10个厂商、5种架构的21个IoT固件镜像上评估，FirmCure实现了100%的网络端口开放率和90.5%的服务交互性，显著优于现有基线方法，干预策略在异构固件间具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: FirmCure成功复现已知漏洞并发现了新的安全缺陷，验证了LLM驱动的全系统重托管框架在自动化和适应性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Full-system rehosting plays a critical role in the security analysis of Linux-based firmware. It matches commonly deployed firmware with sufficient background knowledge. However, for custom devices, existing approaches struggle to handle initialization and runtime obstacles in the rehosting process caused by specialized architectures and hardware-dependent configuration, which heavily rely on expert intervention. This ultimately creates fundamental bottlenecks and results in low rehosting efficiency. To address the above challenges, we propose FirmCure, the first LLM-driven full-system rehosting framework designed for autonomous and adaptive rehosting of Linux-based firmware. FirmCure develops an Adaptive Perception Inference mechanism to extract firmware structural dependencies via static analysis, followed by a Reflective Synthesis module for iterative configuration optimization, and finally an Autonomous Runtime Intervention module for real-time error remediation through runtime fault diagnosis and monitoring. We evaluated 21 IoT firmware images from 10 vendors across 5 architectures, while FirmCure achieved a 100% network port opening rate and 90.5% service interactivity, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Our experiments confirm that FirmCure's intervention strategies generalize across heterogeneous firmware. The framework successfully reproduces known vulnerabilities and discovers new security flaws.

</details>


### [200] [PowerFuzz: Power-Based Black-Box Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24692)
*Dakshina Tharindu,Sahan Sanjaya,Philip Baptist,Prabhat Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PowerFuzz 提出了一种利用功率侧信道测量替代二进制插桩的统计黑盒模糊测试框架，通过滑动窗口和增长窗口全迹相关方法识别执行分支并构建控制流图，在无需内部可见性的情况下实现了接近灰盒模糊测试的分支覆盖率（差距在13.5%以内），相比现有黑盒模糊器提升达22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灰盒模糊测试依赖系统内部可见性（如分支覆盖率反馈），但在固件或二进制不可公开获取的场景下无法应用；黑盒模糊测试缺乏有效的覆盖引导机制，导致效率低下。

Method: 利用功率侧信道迹线替代传统二进制插桩，采用滑动窗口和增长窗口全迹相关方法识别测试执行过程中触发的分支，并据此构建目标固件的高层级控制流图，用于引导模糊器探索未覆盖的执行路径。

Result: 在三个嵌入式硬件平台和十个固件基准测试上的评估表明，PowerFuzz 的分支覆盖率与灰盒模糊器差距在13.5%以内，且显著优于现有黑盒模糊器，提升幅度高达22%。

Conclusion: 功率侧信道信息可有效替代二进制插桩作为黑盒固件模糊测试的反馈信号，实现接近灰盒水平的覆盖引导能力。

Abstract: Fuzzing is widely used for software and hardware verification, offering an effective alternative to random testing. While gray-box fuzzers benefit from full visibility into the system under test and can leverage execution feedback such as branch coverage, these approaches are not applicable when verifying systems whose firmware or binaries are not publicly available. In such scenarios, obtaining coverage information for guiding the fuzzer becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce PowerFuzz, a statistical black-box fuzzing framework that leverages power side-channel measurements as a substitute for binary instrumentation, requiring no internal visibility into the target firmware. A central challenge in black-box firmware fuzzing is determining the executed branches during test execution. To address this challenge, we use power traces to identify branches utilizing a sliding window followed by a growing window full-trace correlation method. This approach also enables the construction of a high-level control-flow graph of the black-box firmware, which we utilize to drive the fuzzer to unexplored execution paths. Extensive evaluation using three embedded hardware platforms and ten firmware benchmarks demonstrates that PowerFuzz can provide branch coverage comparable (within 13.5%) to gray-box fuzzers while significantly outperforming (up to 22%) state-of-the-art black-box fuzzers.

</details>


### [201] [Burnyard: Future of Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24778)
*Rama Ramana Sharma Parnandi,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Burnyard是一个轻量级二进制仿真平台，通过捕获可观察的运行时行为并记录为结构化CSV事件轨迹，解决了传统沙箱分析中资源消耗大、耗时长以及样本可能被意外暴露到公共平台的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统沙箱分析存在两个主要问题：1) 在大型数据中心隔离虚拟机中执行可疑二进制文件时，可能意外将样本暴露给VirusTotal和MalwareBazaar等公共平台；2) 沙箱分析资源密集且耗时。因此需要一种更轻量、更安全的替代方案。

Method: Burnyard采用轻量级二进制仿真平台，通过仿真执行可疑二进制文件，捕获其可观察的运行时行为，并将这些行为记录为结构化的CSV事件轨迹。

Result: Burnyard成功实现了轻量级的恶意软件行为分析，能够以结构化CSV事件轨迹的形式记录运行时行为，同时避免了传统沙箱的资源密集性和样本暴露风险。

Conclusion: Burnyard为恶意软件分析提供了一种更高效、更安全的替代方案，通过轻量级仿真而非完整虚拟机执行，降低了资源消耗和时间成本，同时避免了样本意外泄露到公共平台的风险。

Abstract: Malware analysis is a critical aspect of modern cybersecurity. The prevailing industry practice, sandboxing, involves executing suspicious binaries within isolated virtual machines in large-scale data centers. However, this approach can unintentionally expose samples to public platforms such as VirusTotal and MalwareBazaar, and it is both resource-intensive and time-consuming.
  Burnyard addresses these limitations through a lightweight binary emulation platform that captures observable runtime behavior and records it as structured CSV event traces.

</details>


### [202] [HelpBench: Assessing the Ability of LLMs to Provide Privacy, Safety, and Security Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24819)
*Sarah Meiklejohn,Sunny Consolvo,Patrick Gage Kelley,Tara Matthews,Sai Teja Peddinti,Renee Shelby,Lenin Simicich,Kurt Thomas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HelpBench，一个评估LLM在数字隐私、安全和防护方面提供准确帮助能力的基准。该基准包含450个真实用户场景问题，并开发了评分标准来评估回答的事实准确性和语气。通过自动评分器评估18个最先进LLM的结果显示，虽然模型平均得分82%（高质量建议），但十分之一的回复得分低于65%，反映出不准确甚至有害的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在提供数字隐私、安全和防护建议方面缺乏系统性评估，而这些领域的不准确建议可能对用户造成实际伤害。研究者希望通过构建专门基准来识别模型在这些关键领域的不足之处。

Method: 1) 策划了450个代表真实用户场景的数字隐私/安全问题；2) 为每个问题开发了评估回答事实准确性和语气的评分标准；3) 构建了自动评分器；4) 使用该自动评分器评估了18个最先进LLM的回答质量。

Result: 模型平均得分82%（高质量建议），但10%的回复得分低于65%，反映出存在不准确甚至有害的建议。

Conclusion: 解决模型在数字隐私、安全和防护建议方面的失败案例至关重要，只有这样才能使LLM成为该领域值得信赖的帮助来源。

Abstract: This paper introduces HelpBench, a benchmark for assessing whether LLMs are capable of providing accurate help in response to questions about digital privacy, safety, and security. We curated 450 questions representing authentic user situations and developed rubrics for each question to evaluate the factual accuracy and tone of a response. Example questions touch on how to regain access to lost or suspended accounts, how to balance the trade-offs of hardware security keys versus other forms of two-factor authentication, whether a suspicious email is likely a scam, or whether an abuser might be able to track an individual based on their device peripherals. We then developed and applied an auto-rater to evaluate responses from 18 state-of-the-art LLMs. Our results indicate that while models provide high-quality advice (with scores of 82% on average), one in ten responses from models scores less than 65%, reflecting inaccurate and even harmful advice. Addressing these failures is critical for models to serve as trustworthy sources of assistance for digital privacy, safety, and security needs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Automated Residual Plot Assessment With the R Package autovi and the Shiny Application autovi.web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24236)
*Weihao Li,Dianne Cook,Emi Tanaka,Susan VanderPlas,Klaus Ackermann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个R包，利用计算机视觉模型自动评估线性模型的残差图，并提供Shiny应用辅助分析师判断模型拟合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统残差图可视化评估依赖人工判断，存在可扩展性差、分析人员之间决策不一致的问题。虽然lineup协议可以减少主观性，但需要更多人力投入，因此需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 开发了一个R包，集成计算机视觉模型来自动评估残差图。模型输出视觉信号强度（VSS）并提供辅助信息。同时提供Shiny应用以降低使用门槛。

Result: 模型能够根据残差样本预测视觉信号强度（VSS），并提供支持性信息帮助分析师评估模型拟合情况。

Conclusion: 自动化残差图评估可以有效替代人工判断，提升可扩展性和一致性，适合当前数据驱动的应用场景。

Abstract: Visual assessment of residual plots is a common approach for diagnosing linear models, but it relies on manual evaluation, which does not scale well and can lead to inconsistent decisions across analysts. The lineup protocol, which embeds the observed plot among null plots, can reduce subjectivity but requires even more human effort. In today's data-driven world, such tasks are well suited for automation. We present a new R package that uses a computer vision model to automate the evaluation of residual plots. An accompanying Shiny application is provided for ease of use. Given a sample of residuals, the model predicts a visual signal strength (VSS) and offers supporting information to help analysts assess model fit.

</details>


### [204] [Model selection with proper scoring rules on data sets of time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24715)
*Giorgio Corani,Stefano Damato,Dario Azzimonti,Lorenzo Zambon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列数据上概率模型选择问题，发现均值分数、中位数分数和平均秩这三种模型选择标准在短测试集上会产生冲突，原因在于分数分布的偏态性；随着测试集增大，不同标准逐渐收敛到相同结论，但短测试集下仅均值分数能正确识别真实模型。在间歇性时间序列（包括M5竞赛数据）上的实验强调了大数据集的重要性，并发现平均秩标准对缩放因子不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型选择研究中，均值分数、中位数分数和平均秩这三种聚合统计量在时间序列模型选择时可能给出冲突的决策，但造成这种冲突的根本原因尚未被系统阐明。本文旨在揭示冲突源于分数分布的偏态性，并分析测试集长度对模型选择一致性的影响。

Method: 基于概率模型和时间序列数据，定义评分规则下的分数（score）为单个时间序列测试集上的平均评分值。通过均值分数、中位数分数和平均秩三种统计量进行模型选择，分析分数分布的偏态性对选择结果的影响，并在不同测试集长度下比较三种标准的表现。使用间歇性时间序列（包括M5竞赛数据）进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 三种模型选择标准的冲突源于分数分布的偏态性；2) 随着测试集增大，不同标准逐渐收敛到相同结论；3) 短测试集下，仅均值分数能正确识别真实模型作为最优；4) 平均秩标准对缩放因子不敏感。

Conclusion: 在时间序列模型选择中，均值分数在短测试集下更为可靠，而大数据集（长测试集）能促使不同标准达成一致性。平均秩标准的缩放不变性是其独特性质。研究为实际应用中的模型选择标准选取提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider the problem of model selection between probabilistic models on data sets of time series. Chosen a proper scoring rule, we denote by the term \textit{score} the average value of the scoring rule on the test of an individual time series. For model selection, we need aggregating the values of the scores across multiple time series. Three summary statistics are commonly used for model selection: mean score, median score, and mean rank. Results in previous papers show that these statistics can yield conflicting decisions; we show how the conflicting conclusions are due to the skewness of the distribution of scores. We also show that as the test set of each time series of the data set increases, the different model selection criteria progressively converge to the same conclusion. However, for short tests sets, only the mean score identifies the true model as the best.
  We illustrate these phenomena with an analysis on intermittent time series, including the data set of the M5 competition, where we underline the importance of having a large test set. In such experiments, we further notice that model selection based on mean ranks remains unchanged using different scaling factors.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Order restricted estimation of the parameter functions in an additive hazard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23882)
*Dragi Anevski,ElBatoul Manel Merai*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对Aalen加性风险回归模型中的参数函数，提出了将普通最小二乘估计的朴素估计量通过逐分量l²投影到单调函数空间上得到的估计量，并证明了该估计量具有n^{-1/3}的收敛速度和Chernoff极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Aalen加性风险模型中，传统的普通最小二乘估计量可能不满足单调性约束（如累积风险函数应单调非减），因此需要提出能够保证单调性的改进估计量。

Method: 将Aalen加性风险模型中的普通最小二乘估计（朴素估计量）通过个体化且逐分量的l²投影，投影到单调函数空间上，从而得到满足单调性约束的估计量。

Result: 所提估计量具有逐点极限分布，收敛速度为n^{-1/3}，极限分布为Chernoff分布。

Conclusion: 提出的投影估计量在保持单调性约束的同时，具有非标准的收敛速度和极限分布，为Aalen加性风险模型中的单调函数估计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper we propose estimators of the parameter functions in an Aalen additive hasard regression model. The estimators are the individual and componentwise $l^2$ projections of the naive estimators resulting from the ordinary least squares estimator in the Aalen additive hazard model on the space of monotone functions. We provide pointwise limit distribution results for the resulting estimators, that exhibit $n^{-1/3}$ rate of convergence and the Chernoff distribution as the limit distribution.

</details>


### [206] [Explicit confidence bands and intervals for distribution functions and their derivatives via random Weierstrass-type operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24345)
*José A. Adell,J. T. Alcalá,C. Sangüesa*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文通过随机Weierstrass型算子（特别是随机Steklov算子）重新诠释经典核估计，利用Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式等工具，在仅假设分布函数F的第k阶导数一致连续或存在第一类间断点的条件下，建立了非渐近的显式置信带和置信区间，其长度由F^{(k)}的二阶光滑模决定，当F为次数不超过k+1的多项式时长度为O(n^{-1/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二阶核估计的置信区间通常依赖渐近理论，缺乏非渐近的显式结果。本文旨在建立仅基于光滑性假设（无需额外正则条件）的精确非渐近置信带和置信区间。

Method: 将核估计解释为随机Weierstrass型算子（特别是随机Steklov算子），利用Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式建立置信带，通过子序随机过程的集中不等式或均方误差的精确估计来推导置信区间。

Result: 在F^{(k)}一致连续时给出了置信带；在F^{(k)}存在第一类孤立间断点时给出了中点函数的置信区间。置信带和区间的长度由F^{(k)}的二阶光滑模决定，当F为次数不超过k+1的多项式时长度为O(n^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: 提供的非渐近置信带和置信区间仅依赖于F^{(k)}的一致连续性或第一类间断点假设，长度由光滑性度量决定，在一定条件下达到最优阶O(n^{-1/2})。

Abstract: Classical kernel estimators of second order are interpreted in terms of random Weierstrass-type operators, particularly random Steklov operators. This leads us to obtain explicit nonasymptotic confidence bands and intervals for distribution functions $F$ and their derivatives $F^{(k)}$. Under the only assumption that $F^{(k)}$ is uniformly continuous, confidence bands for $F^{(k)}$ are established by using the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality. To give confidence intervals, we allow $F^{(k)}$ to have isolated discontinuities of the first kind, so that we really estimate the midpoint function $(F^{(k)})_{\star}(x)$. The proofs are based either on concentration inequalities for subordinated stochastic processes or accurate estimates of the MSE of the corresponding estimators. The length of the confidence bands and intervals depends on the degree of smoothness of $F^{(k)}$ measured in terms of the second modulus of continuity. Both lengths are of order $n^{-1 / 2}$ if $F$ is locally a polynomial of degree $k+1$ at most.

</details>


### [207] [Ferguson's Dirichlet Process Breakthrough: A Lasting Legacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24519)
*Antonio Lijoi,Igor Pruenster,Junyi Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Ferguson于1973年提出的Dirichlet过程的三种互补构造方法（有限维分布、gamma过程归一化、预测分布），并指出该过程解决了自1930年代de Finetti以来贝叶斯非参数统计中长期缺乏可处理非参数先验的关键问题，奠定了现代贝叶斯非参数方法的基础。作者建议将Dirichlet过程重新命名为Ferguson-Dirichlet过程以表彰其贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决贝叶斯非参数统计中缺乏既具有大支撑又具有分析可处理性的概率测度空间上的先验这一长期未解决的开放问题。

Method: 综述了Dirichlet过程的三种互补构造：（1）通过有限维Dirichlet分布逼近；（2）通过Gamma过程的归一化；（3）通过预测分布（Polya urn方案）。

Result: Dirichlet过程成为贝叶斯非参数方法论的基石，同时激发了从归一化随机测度到Gibbs型先验等更广泛非参数先验领域的发展。

Conclusion: Ferguson的贡献解决了自1930年代以来贝叶斯非参数框架中的关键障碍，鉴于这一决定性进展，建议将Dirichlet过程重新命名为Ferguson-Dirichlet过程。

Abstract: Ferguson's 1973 introduction of the Dirichlet process marked a breakthrough in Bayesian nonparametric statistics. For the first time, a prior on the space of probability measures fulfilled two key desiderata: large support and analytical tractability. In this paper, we review three complementary constructions of the Dirichlet process, whose roots can be traced back to Ferguson: through finite-dimensional distributions, via normalization of a gamma process, and through predictive distributions. Each perspective not only deepens the understanding of the Dirichlet process but also provides a template for generalizations, from normalized random measures with independent increments to Gibbs--type priors and beyond. Over the past fifty years, the Dirichlet process has become the cornerstone of Bayesian nonparametric methodology and applications, while simultaneously inspiring the expansion of the landscape of nonparametric priors. Since de Finetti laid out the Bayesian nonparametric framework in the 1930s, the key obstacle had been the absence of a tractable nonparametric prior. Ferguson's contribution overcame this challenge, providing a solution to a decades-long open problem. In recognition of this decisive advance, it seems appropriate to refer to the Dirichlet process as the Ferguson--Dirichlet process.

</details>


### [208] [A Concentration Inequality for the Covariance Matrix of an Arbitrary Subset of Random Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24766)
*Huikang Liu,Peng Wang,Laura Balzano*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了由数据依赖子集构成的样本协方差矩阵的谱集中性，针对i.i.d.高斯向量证明了特征值的上下界，并推广到次高斯和弱依赖情形，最后应用于K子空间聚类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典协方差矩阵集中不等式要求随机向量与选择规则独立，但实际应用中（如聚类）样本选择往往依赖于数据本身，破坏了独立性假设，亟需适用于数据依赖子集的谱集中性界。

Method: 对i.i.d.高斯随机向量，利用高斯过程与浓度不等式推导了数据依赖子集样本协方差矩阵最小和最大特征值的高概率上下界；通过更精细的论证替代简单的union-bound，获得更紧的保证；并扩展至次高斯向量和几何强混合高斯序列。

Result: 相比直接union-bound方法，本文结果在子集比例很小时仍能提供显著更紧的谱集中性界；在低秩高斯混合模型的K子空间聚类问题中，聚类误差随信噪比多项式衰减。

Conclusion: 本文建立了数据依赖子集下样本协方差矩阵的谱集中不等式，填补了该方向的理论空白，并为后续数据依赖选择场景的高维统计分析提供了基础工具。

Abstract: Concentration inequalities for sample covariance matrices are fundamental tools in high-dimensional probability. Classical results typically assume that the selected random vectors are independent of the selection rule. In this paper, we study spectral concentration for sample covariance matrices formed from arbitrary, possibly data-dependent subsets of i.i.d. random vectors. Such data-dependent selection destroys the usual independence structure and makes standard covariance concentration bounds inapplicable. For i.i.d. Gaussian random vectors, we prove high-probability lower and upper bounds for the minimal and maximal eigenvalues of such selected covariance matrices. Compared with a direct union-bound argument, our results provide substantially sharper guarantees and allow much smaller subset proportions. We further discuss extensions from Gaussian to sub-Gaussian random vectors, and beyond independence to weakly dependent observations, with geometrically strong-mixing Gaussian sequences serving as a representative example of the latter. Finally, we apply the developed concentration inequalities to the K-subspace clustering problem under a low-rank Gaussian mixture model, where the optimal clusters are inherently data-dependent. Our results yield recovery guarantees showing that the clustering error of global minimizers decays polynomially with the signal-to-noise ratio.

</details>


### [209] [Strong duality for the GROW criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24768)
*Ashwin Ram,Martin Larsson,Johannes Ruf,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了在复合假设检验中通过"对赌"进行检验的通用强对偶性结果。我们刻画了GROW准则（最大对数最优增长）和REGROW准则下的最优e-变量，证明有界e-变量的GROW值总是等于一个弱-*联合信息投影对之间的相对熵，无需对原假设或备择假设施加任何限制。结果推广了Larsson等人从简单备择假设到复合备择假设的工作，并与Larsson的另一项工作形成了对偶类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Larsson等人关于GROW准则（最优增长率）的结果从简单备择假设推广到任意复合备择假设，并建立更一般的强对偶性理论，同时探索有界与无界e-变量情形下的适用条件。

Method: 通过引入弱-*联合信息投影对的概念，利用信息论与凸分析中的对偶方法，分别对有界e-变量和无界e-变量情形下的GROW与REGROW准则进行刻画。

Result: 证明了在任意复合原假设和备择假设下，有界e-变量的GROW值等于弱-*联合信息投影对之间的相对熵；REGROW准则有类似的强对偶性。无界情形需要额外假设，否则推广失败。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一类新的假设检验强对偶定理，将最优e-变量理论与信息投影理论密切联系，并明确了有界与无界情形之间的关键区别。

Abstract: This paper presents general strong duality results when testing hypotheses by betting against them. A bet is an e-variable for a composite null hypothesis $\mathcal{P}$: a nonnegative random variable $X$ whose expected value is at most one under every $¶\in \Pcal$. Following Kelly, Breiman, Cover, Shafer, Grünwald and others, we study a natural minimax \emph{log-optimality} criterion: given a composite alternative $\Qcal$, we characterize the ``GROW value'' $\sup_{X} \inf_{\Q} \E_{\Q}[\log X]$. This paper generalizes the results of \cite{larsson2025numeraire} from (arbitrary $\Pcal$ and) simple $\Qcal$ to arbitrary $\Qcal$. We identify a weak-$*$ joint information projection pair between arbitrary $\Pcal$ and $\Qcal$ that always exists and show that the GROW value for \emph{bounded} e-variables always equals the relative entropy of this pair, without any restrictions on $\Pcal$ or $\Qcal$. We also prove a similarly general strong duality for the REGROW criterion with bounded e-variables and arbitrary bounded offsets. Under various assumptions our results extend to unbounded e-variables, and examples show that without any assumptions such extensions fail. Our results are analogous to those in~\cite{larsson2026complete}, swapping tests for bounded e-variables, minimax risk for the GROW criterion, and total variation for relative entropy.

</details>


### [210] [Autoregressive Processes on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24771)
*Meshal Abuqrais,Davide Pigoli*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黎曼自回归（R-AR）模型，将经典离散时间随机过程推广到流形值数据。模型基于两个参数：Fréchet均值μ（内在中心趋势）和自回归参数φ（控制平稳性和遍历性）。为解决依赖结构参数估计问题，建立了适定度量空间中遍历马尔可夫链样本Fréchet均值的强大数定律，超越经典i.i.d.理论。通过双曲平面数值模拟和Fisher-Rao流形上气溶胶粒径分布应用验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归模型主要针对欧几里得空间数据设计，无法直接处理流形值数据（如形状、方向、概率分布等非线性几何结构数据）。需要建立适用于流形数据内在几何结构的自回归模型，并解决依赖数据下参数估计的理论基础问题。

Method: 提出R-AR(1)模型，参数μ为Fréchet均值（中心趋势），φ为自回归参数（控制平稳性/遍历性）。为克服依赖数据挑战，建立了适定度量空间中遍历马尔可夫链样本Fréchet均值的强大数定律（SLLN），为估计量强相合性提供理论基础。

Result: 建立了遍历马尔可夫链在适定度量空间上样本Fréchet均值的强大数定律，证明了所提R-AR模型参数估计量的强相合性。在双曲平面数值模拟中验证了模型表现，并在Fisher-Rao流形上成功应用于气溶胶粒径分布数据的均值回归动力学刻画。

Conclusion: R-AR模型成功将经典自回归框架扩展到流形值数据，所建立的依赖数据下Fréchet均值的强大数定律为流形上时间序列分析提供了关键理论基础。该框架能够有效刻画非线性几何空间中的均值回归动态，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a Riemannian autoregressive (R-AR) model of order one, generalising classical discrete-time stochastic processes to manifold-valued data. The model is based on two parameters, a parameter $μ$ representing the intrinsic central tendency as the Fréchet mean and an autoregressive parameter $φ$ controlling the stationarity and ergodic properties. Due to the inherent dependence structure of the R-AR process, the estimation procedure for these parameters necessitates new asymptotic results for dependent processes on manifolds. Thus, we establish a strong law of large numbers for the sample Fréchet mean set of ergodic Markov chains in proper metric spaces. By proving this general consistency result, we move beyond the limitations of classical i.i.d. theory to provide the mathematical foundation required for the strong consistency of our proposed estimators. The framework is validated through numerical simulations in the hyperbolic plane and an application to aerosol size distributions on the Fisher-Rao manifold, demonstrating how the proposed model can characterise mean-reverting dynamics in nonlinear geometries.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)
*Yashkumar R Lukhi,Harsh Rameshbhai Moradiya,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化大规模搜索管道，用于在LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中搜索异构4专家混合专家（MoE4）架构。在28天的RTX 4090上进行了搜索，生成了4,463个候选模型，但发现由于itertools.combinations的字母顺序枚举，整个搜索空间（理论23,751种组合的4.8%）完全锚定在单一家族AirNet上。在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3 consistently产生最高精度的集成（平均精度最高0.632），而FractalNet和MNASNet被识别为低效家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化大规模搜索异构MoE架构，替代手动设计，探索LEMUR数据集中基础架构组合的有效性。

Method: 构建确定性代码组装生成器，系统组合LEMUR数据库中的基础架构家族形成MoE4集成，每个集成由卷积门控网络（含温度缩放、mixup增强、余弦退火学习率调度）控制。在NVIDIA RTX 4090上进行28天搜索，生成4,463个候选模型。

Result: 发现了覆盖偏差：因itertools.combinations的字母顺序枚举，整个搜索空间锚定在AirNet家族。ShuffleNet+MobileNetV3组合达成最高平均精度0.632。FractalNet和MNASNet为低效家族。

Conclusion: 识别了搜索空间覆盖偏差的根因，提出分层随机抽样修复方案，并开源管道及分析产物。

Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

</details>


### [212] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种离线强化学习损失函数（SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO）在数学推理蒸馏中的机制差异。发现SFT、RFT、RIFT的权重更新几乎共线，DFT偏离更大，Offline GRPO在SFT方向上增加正交分量，而DPO处于近正交子空间且存在模式连通性障碍。DPO取得了最高准确率（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%），但学习率比其他方法小10倍，公平比较有待后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅从下游准确率比较各类离线强化学习损失函数，缺乏对其更新机制差异的理解。本文旨在探究这些方法在权重更新方向上是否存在本质区别，而不仅仅是最终性能差异。

Method: 以Qwen3-4B为基础模型，使用attention-only LoRA，在相同数学推理rollout数据上训练六种方法（SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO）。通过余弦相似度、主角子空间分析、线性模式连通性和CKA等指标分析权重增量（delta）的几何关系。

Result: (i) SFT、RFT、RIFT的权重增量几乎共线（余弦≥0.97，主角中位数~7°），GSM8K准确率相当（87-88%）；(ii) DFT的方向偏离大于任何加权方法；(iii) Offline GRPO在SFT方向上有显著正交分量（全局~67%，深层最高~86%），但仍在SFT损失盆地内；(iv) DPO处于近正交子空间，存在模式连通性障碍，后期层CKA降至~0.46。DPO达到最高准确率（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%），但其学习率小10倍。

Conclusion: 不同离线强化学习方法在权重更新机制上存在显著差异，不能仅从下游准确率等同视之。DPO取得了最佳性能但学习率差异混淆了比较，需要后续进行学习率匹配的公平对比。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

</details>


### [213] [A Survey on Federated Causal Discovery and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23741)
*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果发现（FCD）与联邦因果推断（FCI）领域，通过多维分类法组织现有研究，阐明了二者作为统一联邦因果推理流水线中互补阶段的关系，并指出了隐私、通信效率、理论保证等共同挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理对数据驱动决策至关重要，但可靠因果分析所需的数据往往分布在不同机构且因隐私或通信约束无法集中。联邦学习虽能解决这一问题，但联邦因果发现与推断领域跨学科性强且缺乏系统性综述，给研究者带来入门障碍。

Method: 提出多维分类法：将FCD沿三个核心设计决策（结构学习方式、数据划分方式、各方获得的结构知识）组织为三个轴（方法学范式、联邦拓扑、结构范围）；进一步考察时序动态、数据异质性、缺失数据、非一致变量集等实践维度。对FCI，按目标估计量（平均vs个体化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（从经典加权到现代深度生成架构）分类。最后统一论述FCD与FCI的互补关系及共同挑战。

Result: 建立了FCD与FCI的系统性分类体系，明确了二者作为统一联邦因果推理流水线中互补阶段（FCD提供结构知识用于FCI效应估计）的形式化连接，并梳理了隐私、通信效率、理论保证、应用领域等共享关注点。

Conclusion: 联邦因果发现与推断是一个快速发展的交叉领域，现有工作将两者分离处理，本文通过统一视角揭示其内在联系，并指出开放挑战（如隐私保护、通信效率、理论保证等）为未来研究提供方向。

Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers. This paper bridges that gap by providing a systematic review through multi-dimensional taxonomies. Grounded in the three core design decisions underlying any FCD solution, namely how structures are learned, how data are partitioned, and what structural knowledge each party obtains, we organize FCD along three axes: methodological paradigm, federation topology, and structural scope. We further examine key practical dimensions, including temporal dynamics, data heterogeneity, missing data, and non-identical variable sets. For FCI, we categorize methods by target estimand (average versus individualized/conditional treatment effects) and by estimation strategy, from classical weighting methods to modern deep generative architectures. Unlike prior works that treat FCD and FCI separately, we formalize their connection as complementary stages of a unified federated causal reasoning pipeline, where FCD supplies the structural knowledge required for valid effect estimation in FCI. Finally, we highlight their shared concerns regarding privacy, communication efficiency, theoretical guarantees, and application domains, and conclude by identifying open challenges for future research.

</details>


### [214] [Low-power analogue neural networks with trainable nonlinear connections for continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23742)
*Ian T. Vidamour,Fernando Aguirre,Thomas J. Hayward,Matthew O. A. Ellis,Charles Swindells,Alexander McDonnell,Martin Trefzer,Finley Robins,Luca Manneschi,Susan Stepney,Tony Kenyon,Oliver J. Sutton,Jack C. Gartside,Ivan Y. Tyukin,Adnan Mehonic,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在物理神经网络中将可训练的非线性函数置于连接上（而非节点上），受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发，利用模拟带通滤波器在现场可编程模拟阵列上实现。实验表明，该方法在表示平滑连续值目标（如机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪）时，比多层感知机需要更少的节点和连接，但在分类决策边界上无参数效率优势。约35,000个连接成功迁移至硬件，专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦，忆阻器仿真复现了相同行为，表明优势源于连接上的可训练非线性而非具体器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理神经网络将非线性器件响应强制作为标量权重，限制了表达能力。本文旨在通过将可训练非线性函数置于连接上，提升物理神经网络在平滑连续值任务上的参数效率，并探索不同物理平台（模拟阵列、CMOS、忆阻器）的通用可行性。

Method: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发，将可训练非线性函数放置在网络的连接上，而非节点上；利用现场可编程模拟阵列（FPAA）上的模拟带通滤波器实现这些非线性函数；训练后的网络迁移至硬件，并在CMOS和忆阻器平台上进行仿真验证。

Result: 该方法在平滑连续值任务（机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪）上远超MLP的参数效率，但在分类决策边界上无优势。约35,000个连接成功迁移至硬件，专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30μW，忆阻器仿真复现了相同行为。

Conclusion: 物理神经网络中连接上的可训练非线性函数带来的优势是任务依赖的，源于物理基函数的平滑性，而非特定器件实现。该方法为实现低功耗、高效能的模拟机器学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Physical neural networks promise low-power machine learning by computing directly with analogue device physics, but most architectures force nonlinear device responses to act as scalar weights. Inspired by Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we place trainable nonlinear functions on the connections, making each physical connection a learnable computational element. Realising these functions as analogue band-pass filters on field-programmable analogue arrays, we find that the benefit is task-dependent and follows from the smoothness of the physical basis: the networks represent smooth, continuously valued targets, including robotic kinematics, continuous control, and photovoltaic maximum-power-point tracking, with far fewer nodes and connections than multilayer perceptrons, but offer no parameter-efficiency advantage on classification-like decision boundaries. Trained networks transfer to hardware across approximately 35,000 connections with quantified fidelity, and a dedicated CMOS implementation is projected to operate at approximately 30 microwatts. A memristive realisation reproduces the same behaviour in simulation, indicating that the advantage comes from placing trainable nonlinearity on connections, rather than from a particular device.

</details>


### [215] [Synergizing Physically Constrained MCMC and Chemical-Informed Gaussian Processes for Reaction Network Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23757)
*Runzhe Liu,Zihao Wang,Wenbo Yang,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PC-MCMC-CIGP工作流，整合了尖峰-板层拓扑采样、硬守恒与热力学筛选以及化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型，用于从稀疏、含噪的化学反应时序数据中提取可解释的控制方程，并在H2+Br2基准和苯乙烯环氧化反应中验证了其性能，相比GP-BO基线在最终产率上提升了12.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、含噪的化学反应时序数据中提取可解释的控制方程具有挑战性，因为离散反应拓扑与连续动力学参数紧密耦合，现有方法难以区分真实的基元反应路径与欺骗性的唯象拟合。

Method: 提出了一种可复现的灰箱工作流PC-MCMC-CIGP，包括：(1) 尖峰-板层拓扑采样进行反应网络结构推断；(2) 硬守恒与热力学筛选施加物理约束；(3) 化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型用于参数校准和实验设计；(4) 多种采集函数（EI、GWU、PC-EI、不确定性采样、差异追踪、随机搜索）用于主动学习。

Result: 在H2+Br2基准上，约束采样器能够区分基元自由基路径与欺骗性唯象拟合；在苯乙烯环氧化反应上，CIGP优化循环使最终产率比GP-BO基线提升12.5%；10种子采集策略研究表明，PC-EI显著减少了低产率的贝叶斯优化建议，而EI类准则在最终产率性能上最强。

Conclusion: 该工作流的核心贡献不在于提出新的MCMC或GP系列，而在于将这些组件整合为物理约束驱动的、具有显式不确定性感知采集选择的灰箱工作流，能够更可靠地从化学时序数据中提取控制方程并优化反应条件。

Abstract: Extracting interpretable governing equations from sparse, noisy chemical time-series data remains difficult because discrete reaction topology and continuous kinetic parameters are tightly coupled. We present PC-MCMC-CIGP, a reproducible gray-box workflow that combines spike-and-slab topology sampling, hard conservation and thermodynamic screening, and a Chemical-Informed Gaussian Process (CIGP) residual model for parameter calibration and experimental design. The methodological contribution is not a new MCMC or GP family in isolation; rather, it is the integration of these components into a physically constrained workflow with explicit uncertainty-aware acquisition choices. On the H2 + Br2 benchmark, the constrained sampler distinguishes elementary radical pathways from deceptive phenomenological fits in our experiments. On styrene epoxidation, the CIGP optimization loop improves final yield by 12.5% over the reported GP-BO baseline. A new 10-seed acquisition study shows that EI, GWU, PC-EI, uncertainty sampling, discrepancy hunting, and random search have different trade-offs: PC-EI substantially reduces low-yield BO suggestions, while EI-style criteria give the strongest final-yield performance.

</details>
